%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
%%%%%  Final call for computing score for fire.  %%%%%
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

stepLength_tree1(1.0).
stepLength_tree2(1.0).
stepLength_tree3(1.0).
stepLength_tree4(1.0).
stepLength_tree5(1.0).

logPrior(-1.8).
fire(D, Total) :- // A general accessor. 
   fire(D, 1000000, Total), !.
fire(D, Total) :- waitHere("This should not fail", fire(D, Total)).

fire(D, TreesToUse, Total) :- // A tree-limited accessor (e.g., for tuning the number of trees to use).
   logPrior(LogPrior),
   getScore_fire_tree1(D, TreesToUse, Total1),
   getScore_fire_tree2(D, TreesToUse, Total2),
